under Variable value. The software products manage their own files. The file name must be
specified.

HC_HOMESCREEN_NEWS
Use the HC_HOMESCREEN_NEWS environment variable to switch off the news area of
the start screen by setting the value of the variable to 0.
HC_MAX_HOMESCREEN_ENTRIES
The maximum number of recently used documents is set to 20. This can be reduced using
the HC_MAX_HOMESCREEN_ENTRIES environment variable. Already saved, longer file
lists are shortened. The environment variable can be set to 0 to not display the most
recently used documents at all. The names of the most recently used documents are saved
in the registry.
HC_NEW_HOME_SCREEN
Switch the start screen on and off using the HC_NEW_HOME_SCREEN environment varia-
ble. Set the value of the environment variable to 0 for switching off and 1 for switching on.
HC_HOMESCREEN_LOCALE
Change the language of the start screen using theHC_HOMESCREEN_LOCALE environ-
ment variable.
OMSL_COMPANY_SETTINGS
Import company-wide default settings for options/properties, interfaces, snap options, tooltip
content, dimensions, title blocks, frames, feature settings and color libraries.
Set up the Windows environment variable. Enter the OMSL_COMPANY_SETTINGS envi-
ronment variable under Variable name and enter the path, e.g. W:\Companyfolder\ under
Variable value.
The directory structure on the server must be set up in accordance with the following rules.
Use a subdirectory with the software version number as its name to hold the company
settings assigned to a specific software version.

[Companyfolder]

OMSL_GRAPHICS_ENABLE_PIXEL_RATIO
The environment variable OMSL_GRAPHICS_ENABLE_PIXEL_RATIO = 1 extends the
scaling behavior for adjusting the display on a 4k monitor to the 3D rendering window
as well (for graphics areas, manipulators, snap points, workplanes, etc.) using the same
ratio that Qt calculates for invariant zoom objects.
Related topics. Graphics card and monitor (page 571)
OMSL_PRINT_DATE_FORMAT
Customize format for printing date and time.
Use the OMSL_PRINT_DATE_FORMAT environment variable to customise the format for
printing the date and time. The operating system settings for region and language are con-
sidered. You can change these locale settings in Windows under Control Panel → Region
and Language → Formats → Advanced Settings.
Select the format with the following placeholders.
CAD

-- 594 of 622 --

LONG long format
COM-
PACT
short format (default)
Customize the format using the following placeholders.
Date:
d Day of the month as a number (1 - 31).
dd Day of the month as a number with leading zero (01 – 31).
ddd Day of the week, abbreviated (e.g., Mon).
dddd Day of the week, not abbreviated (e.g., Monday).
M Month as a number (1 - 12).
MM Month as a number with leading zero (01 - 12).
MM
M
Name of the month, abbreviated (e.g., Jan).
MM
MM
Name of the month, not abbreviated (e.g., January)
yy Year, two-digit (00 - 99)
yyyy Year, four-digit
Time information:
h Hour (0 - 23 or 1 - 12 with AM / PM display)
hh Hour with leading zero (00 - 23 or 01 - 12 with AM / PM display)
H Hour (0 - 23 even with AM / PM display)
HH Hour with leading zero (00 - 23 even with AM / PM display)
m Minute (0 – 59)
mm Minute with leading zero (00 - 59)
s Second without leading zero (0 - 59)
ss Second with leading zero (00 - 59)
z Millisecond without leading zero (0 - 999)
zzz Millisecond with leading zero (000 - 999)
AP AM / PM display. A / P represents 'AM' and 'PM'.
A AM / PM display. A represents 'AM' and 'PM'.
ap am / pm display. ap represents 'am' and 'pm'.
a am / pm display. a represents 'am' and 'pm'.
QT_AUTO_SCREEN_SCALE_FACTOR
Activate support for high DPI values of a monitor, such as a 4K monitor.
With environment variables QT_AUTO_SCREEN_SCALE_FACTOR = 1, activate the auto-
matic scaling based on the pixel density of the monitor. The size of fonts is not changed in
the point, as the point is a physical unit of measure.
Adjusting title block and frame
Title block and frames are stored in templates in *.hmc format.
The company settings (highest priority), user settings and finally, installation settings (low-
est priority) evaluated for title block and frames. Templates for the title block must be loca-
ted in the Printing title blocks directory. Templates for frames must be located in the Printing
frames directory. All templates are collected. In the event of duplicates, the template from
the directory with the higher priority is provided.
Drawing frames
Enter a frame in the desired shape. The outer contour describes the edge of the page. The
workplane must be on the bottom left corner. Draw another contour for the frame within this
contour. Use a point to specify which corner the title block should be aligned to. Hide this
point.
CAD
581

-- 595 of 622 --

Save the document as a *.hmc file. The file name must comply with the rules, as the
template is assigned using the file name of one of paper sizes selected in the print settings:
Paper size Underscore Freely-select-
able text
Underscore Orientation Extension
For example:
4A0 _ Text _ P .hmc
A4 _ Text _ L .hmc
A4 _ Text _ P .hmc
A _ Text _ L .hmc
It is possible to generate templates for ANSI Y14.1 paper sizes from A to E and the DIN
EN ISO 216 paper sizes of the A to C series from class 4...0, 2...0, 0 to 5. Identify vertical
alignment with the letter P. Identify horizontal alignment with the letter L. The frame from the
template is fitted while maintaining the aspect ratio in the paper size.
Title block
Enter the title block in the desired form.
User defined information can be directly applied in the title block using TAGs. To do so,
perform the following steps in the template:
Create a tag with Tags → Create → Quantified tag.
Determine the position within the title block and insert a link to the tag in Drafting → Text.
Assign the tag to the text in Tags → Assign.
Save the document in the Printing title blocks directory as an *.hmc file. The file name is
freely selectable.
The tag is then displayed in the value table for the Title block when printing.
Font
The software provides its own hyperMILL font.
Updating help files
Help information is output to a browser in HTML5 format. For this purpose, the software
associated with the *.html file extension in the operating system is called. If the file
extension is to be associated with another browser software, it is advisable to use the
omAppSettingsX64.exe software under ...\Info Center\.... This means that the association
only applies to OPEN MIND software. The association for other software on the computer
remains unaffected.
Following procedure is valid up to and including Version 2018.2:
If an older Help version is opened, even though a current Help version is available, delete
the temporary Help files in C:\Users\[USER]\AppData\Local\OPEN MIND\[Software]\help\....
To view the data, select the Folder options – Advanced Settings → Show hidden files,
folders and drives option in Windows. Check the version of the Help displayed on the
homepage.

NLopt
README
NLopt is a library for nonlinear local and global optimization, for functions with and without
gradient information. It is designed as as simple, unified interface and packaging of several
free/open-source nonlinear optimization libraries.
The latest release and a complete manual may be found at the NLopt home page: http://ab-
initio.mit.edu/nlopt
It is compiled and installed with the standard GNU autoconf/automake commands:
./configure
make
make install
See `./configure --help` or the `INSTALL` file for other options. To build the latest develop-
ment sources:
git clone git://github.com/stevengj/nlopt
cd nlopt
sh autogen.sh
make
CAD
593

-- 607 of 622 --

(To build from git, you will need GNU autoconf, automake, and libtool installed, along with
SWIG and Unix tools such as m4, perl, and sed.)
Once it is installed, `#include <nlopt.h>` in your C/C++ programs and link it with `-lnlopt -lm`.
You may need to use the C++ compiler to link in order to include the C++ libraries (which
are used internally by NLopt, even though it has a C API).
The minimization function, `nlopt_minimize`, is described in the [man page](http://en.wiki-
pedia.org/wiki/Man_page) (`api/nlopt_minimize.3`, which is installed by `make install`. See
also the manual on our web page.
There are also interfaces for Fortran, Python, Matlab, Octave, OCaml, GNU Guile, GNU R,
Lua, and Julia. Interfaces for other languages may be added in the future.

Glossary
Software
Icons Otherwise known as symbols or tools in a toolbar, icons
are usually clicked to open or run something.
Modal dialog A modal dialog blocks interaction in another window of
the same software as long as it is open. Dialogs which
allow you to simultaneously work in other windows of
the software are known as modeless.
Multi-screen A computer with two or more monitors.
Open Inventor Open Inventor is an object-oriented C++ program li-
brary used to create 3D graphics with OpenGL.
Regular expressions Regular expressions are a form of pattern matching,
frequently used when processing text data.
The Perl programming language can be used as an
example.
Regular expressions are a complex search tool that
make it possible to intelligently examine text informa-
tion for strings that match certain patterns. The soft-
ware searches for a part of a character string that
matches the specified regular expression. This means
that the system does not check whether the entire text
entered matches the regular expression. A part of the
text or character string is sufficient.
http://perldoc.perl.org/perlre.html#Regular-Expressions
(\d{4}[- ]){3}\d{4} This regular expression searches for
three groups of characters, where each group con-
sists of four figures followed by a hyphen or space,
and finally four additional figures. This could be an
order number or part number, for example. If the fol-
lowing keyword tags have been assigned to various en-
tities: 1234-5678-0000-00000000, 1111 2222 3333CC,
1234-5678-1111-2222, then all of these tags will be
found using the filter condition from the example.
Second-level command A second-level command is a command that does not
interrupt an active command.
Wildcard character Wildcard characters can be inserted in a pattern string
to find a string matching the specified characteristics:
* = Matches any combination of permissible characters.
? = Matches any single permissible character.

Erosion
Raw material A workpiece, usually made from copper, graphite or
tungsten-copper, intended for further processing to be-
come an electrode for electrical discharge machining.
CAD
Boundary The boundary is the edge of a face.
Continuity This value describes the continuity of curve and face
transitions. The software allows a positional transition
(G0), a tangential transition (G1) or a curvature con-
stant transition (G2).
Domain A domain is an initial face in its state before trimming.
Edge An edge arises between two connected boundaries
within a solid.
Feature CAD feature and hyperMILL feature:
hyperMILL feature: Geometries such as holes, pockets,
planes, 2D and 3D profiles are recognized automatical-
ly, both on faces and solid models. These automatically
recognized geometries are known as features. They
are generated with all parameters necessary for further
CAM processing. The recognized features can be fur-
ther processed in many ways. For example, jobs or
cycles can be assigned to the features, or more com-
plex machining sequences in the form of macros can
be generated based on features.
Group Entity to combine other entities in the model tree.
Mil Thou (abbreviation for a thousandth of an inch) is a
length dimension in the imperial and US system of units
and the English name for the American mil unit (from
Lat. millesimus). 1 thou is a thousandth of an inch,
which corresponds to 25.4 μm.
1 mil = 1/1000 in.;
1 mil = 25.4 μm;
1 mil = 25.4 • 10-6 m;
Multishell mesh A multishell mesh is a mesh with isolated triangles not
connected with the main cluster.
NURBS Geometric entity defined using NURBS mathematics.

Polyline Entity that represents a group composed of straight line
segments. Polylines are not a subcategory of curves.
Shape A parent object in the model tree, used to improve the
sorting of mesh, solid, point cloud and face entity types.
Zone A zone is a CAD feature. Any faces inside a solid can
be combined as desired.
